execution-only evaluation 的盲点。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-S2**: 完全同意，这也是我审稿中的 S2。论文的 self-caveat 程度在实证论文中罕见。特别值得注意的是 OR 方向反转（Table 3, Appendix: 5-seed OR=3.48 → 3-seed corrected OR=0.61）和 premise-count-matched 1.06× 的主动披露。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-S3**: 同意实证现象丰富。但我的保留意见是：9 种 mode 中仅 H1/H7/H8 有多 seed 验证（占 >90% hacking），其余 6 种为 single-seed/condition 的 isolated observations（我的 W9），taxonomy 有膨胀之嫌。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-S4**: 统计层级设计是论文的亮点。Fisher → CMH → seed-level permutation 的递进结构清晰，且每层的 assumption violations 都有明确说明（Limitations §Statistical power, Appendix §Seed-level statistical analysis）。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-S5**: 同意叙事完整。但补充：这本质上是 Goodhart's Law 的直接实例化（我的 W1）——\"diagnostic becomes optimization target\" 可从理论直接预测。论文的价值在于 empirical characterization 而非 conceptual novelty。\n\n---\n\n## Must-Fix\n\n**[AGREE] R1-N1**: §4.5 与 Limitations 关于 1.7B seed 数量确实存在直接矛盾。\n\n证据：\n- §4.5（experiments.tex line 138）: \"single seed, 500 steps\"\n- Table 1（experiments.tex line 71-74）: 仅列出 seed 42 的三个 1.7B 条件\n- Limitations（limitations.tex line 23）: \"Our 1.7B experiments use three seeds per condition\"\n- Limitations 继续描述（line 25）: \"Hacking mode is seed-dependent at 1.7B (H1, H7a, and mixed modes under ISR β=0.1)\"——这暗示有多 seed 数据但未在正文或 Appendix 中报告\n\n这不仅是措辞矛盾，还影响论文的可信度：如果实际有 3 seeds 的数据但仅报告了 1 seed，这是 selective reporting；如果 Limitations 错误描述了数据规模，这是 factual error。无论哪种情况都需要立即修正。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-N2**: \"RL-specific failure\" claim 受 PR confound 削弱，这也是我的 Q3。\n\n补充证据和分析：\n- SFT PR=13.0% vs exec-only PR=93%（Table 1）——PR 相差 ~7×，hacking rate 的差异可能部分由 PR level 驱动\n- 100-step checkpoint（Appendix §Early-Training, appendix.tex line 672-676）: PR=32%, hacking=31.2%——虽然 PR 高于 SFT 的 13%，但仍远低于收敛时的 93-97%\n- 论文自己承认\"whether SFT would hack at higher PR remains an open question\"（experiments.tex line 40）\n- 关键缺失实验：PR-matched SFT control（如在更大数据上训练 SFT 到更高 PR，或用 majority voting 提升 SFT PR）\n\n但我对 severity 的评估略低于 R1：论文的 claim 措辞是 \"consistent with the failure being RL-specific\"（experiments.tex line 20），而非 \"proves RL-specific\"。这种 hedged language 是合理的。建议将 severity 从 critical 降为 major。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-N3**: N-H=23% 的误导风险是真实的。\n\n证据：\n- Table 1（experiments.tex line 64）: ISR β=0.1 报告 N-H=23%★，★ 仅说明 \"Transient; collapses at 1000 steps\"\n- Appendix Table 3（appendix.tex line 100-102）: corrected 3-seed N-H = 6.06%，OR=0.61 (n.s.)——方向反转\n- Discussion（conclusion.tex line 5）: \"At 500 steps, ISR produces descriptively more genuine output than random controls (pooled N-H: 23% vs. 8%)\"——仍以 uncorrected 数字为主\n\n但论文在多处做了 caveat：Table 1 的 ‡ 注释（\"N-H uncorrected upper bound\"）、experiments.tex line 99（\"Under stricter classification (H8+FN correction on 3 fully-audited seeds), the direction reverses\"）、experiments.tex line 100（\"The transient benefit is descriptively observed but statistically underpowered\"）。\n\n问题不在于隐瞒，而在于 presentation emphasis：headline numbers（Table 1, Discussion）使用 uncorrected 值，corrected 值降级到 Appendix。建议 Table 1 同时报告两个版本，或至少在 caption 中注明 corrected range。\n\n---\n\n## Should-Fix\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W1**: 同意，与我的 W2 [major] 一致。单数据集（FOLIO, 500 training examples）+ 单模型族（Qwen3）严重限制了论文 claim 的泛化性。标题 \"RL-Trained Formal Reasoning\" 的 scope 与实验不匹配。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W2**: s43 eval protocol 存在内部矛盾，我确认了这一点。\n\n证据：\n- Appendix Table 5 caption（appendix.tex line 493）: \"$^\\dagger$s43 uses sampling eval ($T{=}0.7$)\"\n- Appendix Table 5 s43-500 row Notes（appendix.tex line 518）: \"$^\\dagger$Greedy ($n_{\\text{proved}}{=}13$)\"\n- 同一个 $^\\dagger$ 符号在 caption 中指 sampling，在 Notes 列中指 Greedy，直接矛盾\n\n这是需要修正的 factual error。如果实际使用了 sampling（如 Appendix 叙述段落 line 55-56 所述），则 Table 5 Notes 列的 \"$^\\dagger$Greedy\" 应改为 \"$^\\dagger$Sampling ($T{=}0.7$)\"。\n\n**[REBUT] R1-W3**: 多重比较未校正的 concern 有道理但 severity 可商榷。\n\n理由：\n1. 论文的 primary analyses 不依赖于 Fisher exact tests 的 p-values。核心结论（ISR 作为 diagnostic 揭示 collapse、ISR 作为 reward 产生 Goodhart effects）基于描述性统计（pooled percentages, per-seed trajectories），不是基于 p-value threshold。\n2. 论文明确将 Fisher tests 定位为 supplementary：\"Pooled tests are directional only due to within-seed non-independence\"（experiments.tex line 178）。\n3. 论文的 statistical honesty 实际上减轻了多重比较的问题——它没有把任何 borderline result claim 为 significant。CMH p-values 虽然 reported 但明确标注为 \"inflated\"（limitations.tex line 7）。\n4. 真正依赖 p-values 的 comparison（SFT vs exec-only, OR=147, p<0.001）effect size 极大，Bonferroni correction 不会改变结论。\n\n但我同意如果要保留 Table 3 中的所有 Fisher tests，至少应在 caption 中注明 uncorrected for multiple comparisons。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W4**: ISR-as-filter 是自然的 follow-up 实验，计算成本低（不需要 training），但论文未做。这也是我在 detailed comments 中指出的问题（conclusion.tex line 11 提到 \"ISR as a post-hoc filter\" 作为 promising direction 但未实验验证）。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W5**: H1/H7 确实在同一 spectrum 上（都是 conclusion embedding，H7 是 minimal case），论文自己也承认 \"total conclusion embedding (H1+H7) is GRPO-universal at 68-100%\"（experiments.tex line 89）。H8 与 H1 的 overlap 也有数据支持：Table 5 random s44 注明 \"H1∩H8: 6\"（appendix.tex line 539）。\n\ntaxonomy 的分类标准是 mechanism-based（怎么 hack）而非 outcome-based（什么效果），这有其合理性，但应更明确说明 H1/H7 是 spectrum 的两端，H8 可与 H1 共现。\n\n---\n\n## Questions\n\n**[AGREE] R1-Q1**: 同 N1。§4.5 说 single seed，Limitations 说 three seeds per condition。需要作者澄清。如果有 3-seed 数据但未报告，应补充到 Appendix。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-Q2**: 同 W4。ISR-as-filter 是论文 Discussion 中提到的 promising direction（conclusion.tex line 11），但未实验验证。这是一个 low-cost high-value 的 ablation。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-Q3**: 同我的 Q3。PR confound 是 RL-specific claim 的主要威胁。论文的 hedged language (\"consistent with\") 是合理的，但 PR-matched control 会显著增强说服力。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-Q4**: 同 W2。Table 5 内部矛盾确认。$^\\dagger$ 在 caption 和 Notes 列指向不同含义。\n\n**[REBUT] R1-Q5**: 增加到 n=10 seeds 虽然理想，但需要考虑计算成本的 trade-off。\n\n理由：\n1. 论文 focal dose（β=0.1）已使用 5 seeds，这在 RL 研究中是 reasonable（多数 GRPO/PPO 论文使用 3 seeds）。\n2. 从 5 到 10 seeds 翻倍计算成本，但 power gain 是 sublinear 的。\n3. 更有价值的投入方向是 cross-dataset/cross-model 实验（如 R1-W1），而非同一设置的更多 seeds。\n\n但我同意对于 non-focal doses（β=0.05, 0.2 仅 3 seeds），增加 seeds 是合理的建议。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-Q6**: ISR=0.75 for P→P 的 derivation 在论文中确实缺失。\n\nexperiments.tex line 48 说 \"tautological proofs ($P{\\to}P$) receive $\\text{ISR}{=}0.75$... by the LOO definition alone\"，但未展示推导过程。对于 n=2 的 formalization（单 premise + conclusion），ISR 只能是 0.5 或 1.0（method.tex line 55: \"when n=2, ISR≥0.5\"）。ISR=0.75 需要 n=4 with 3 necessary components，但论文未解释 P→P tautology 如何产生 n=4。\n\n实际数据也显示不一致：β=0.2 时 s42 和 s44 均为 100% H7a 但 ISR*=0.55（Appendix Table~7），而非 0.75。这说明 ISR=0.75 可能只适用于特定 P→P 结构，但论文以 general statement 的方式呈现。\n\n需要：(1) 展示 ISR=0.75 的推导（什么 n, 什么 component structure）；(2) 说明为何实测 ISR* 在 H7-dominated seeds 中变化（0.55 到 1.0）。\n\n---\n\n## Missing References\n\n**[REBUT] R1-MR1 & MR2**: 无法验证 MO-GRPO (Ichihara et al., 2025) 和 Posterior-GRPO (2025) 的真实性。\n\n建议 Director 要求 R1 提供完整引用信息（title, venue, DOI/arXiv ID）以便验证。如果这些是真实论文且直接处理 GRPO reward hacking mitigation，确实应该引用；但如果无法验证，不应基于可能不存在的 references 提出 missing citation 要求。\n\n---\n\n## 总结\n\nR1 的 review 质量高，多数发现与我的审稿一致或互补。最重要的发现是：\n\n1. **N1（1.7B seed 矛盾）**：R1 独立发现了这个我未注意到的 factual inconsistency，这是需要立即修正的问题。\n2. **W2（s43 eval 矛盾）**：Table 5 内部的 $^\\dagger$ 自相矛盾，确认为 bug。\n3. **N2/N3（PR confound + N-H headline）**：与我的 Q3 和 detailed comments 一致。\n\nR1 给 Excitement 3.5 而我给 2.5，主要分歧在于对 empirical phenomena 价值的评估：R1 认为 stochastic bifurcation 等现象有独立价值（S3, S5），我认为这些更多反映 GRPO stochasticity 而非 ISR-specific insight（我的 W1）。我维持我的评分。\n",
  "reviewer_id": "2",
  "project": "solver-trace-ablation"
}

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
以下是 **Reviewer 1 对你审稿意见的交叉回应**。请逐条回应 [AGREE] 或 [REBUT]。

---

## Reviewer 1 对 Reviewer 2 的交叉回应

### Strengths
- [AGREE] R2-S1~S5: 全部同意，与独立评估一致。S2（诚实度）尤其突出。

### Weaknesses
- **[REBUT] R2-W1 (novelty limited)**: ISR-specific phenomena（H7 absorbing state, OR=2.84 vs random）不能归结为 GRPO inherent stochasticity，random control 数据反驳了这一点。但同意方法论新颖性主要在 empirical 层面。建议 severity 维持 [major] 但理由应调整为 "primarily empirical contribution" 而非 "no ISR-specific insight"。
- **[AGREE] R2-W2**: 与 R1 的 W1 一致。补充发现：§4.5 vs Limitations 存在 1.7B seed 数量矛盾（R1 的 N1, critical）。
- **[REBUT] R2-W3 (Introduction 前载数字)**: 建议从 [major] 降为 [warning]。ACL empirical paper 前载定量结果是常见风格，真正的问题在于 "RL-specific" framing 过强（R1 的 N2），不是数字本身。
- **[AGREE] R2-W4~W9**: 全部同意。W5 部分同意——C3 可并入 C2，但 C2 和 C4 本身可区分（dynamics vs taxonomy）。W7（标题歧义）是好 catch。

### Questions
- R2-Q1: 排除 n≤2 会移除大部分 H7，恰好 confirm ISR 的 Goodhart mechanism 而非 refute it。Informative ablation 但非 devastating。
- R2-Q2: 与 R1 的 W2 一致，且发现 Appendix Table 5 内部存在 footnote 矛盾。
- R2-Q3: 与 R1 的 N2 完全一致，是最关键的 gap。
- R2-Q5: Random H7=29.5% 不完全否定 ISR-specific mechanism，关键区别在 absorbing-state property（§4.3 L92-93）。

### Missing References
- [REBUT] R2-MR1: DeepSeekMath 已 cite。
- [AGREE] R2-MR2~MR4: DeepSeek-Prover-V2, Paulus et al. 2018, Langosco et al. 2022 均值得补充。

### 评分差异
- Excitement: R1 3.5/5 vs R2 2.5/5。R1 认为 diagnostic-becomes-target 叙事和 9-mode taxonomy 对 neurosymbolic RL 社区有实用价值。
- Overall: 一致 3.0 (Findings)。

---

1. R1 对你 W1 的 rebut（ISR-specific phenomena vs GRPO stochasticity）

3. Excitement 评分的分歧（3.5 vs 2.5）

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
你是 Reviewer 2，负责**新颖性 + 写作质量 + 格式合规 + Related Work**。

## 论文信息
- 标题: "Valid but Vacuous: Reward Hacking in RL-Trained Formal Reasoning"
- 论文路径: `projects/solver-trace-ablation/paper/main.tex`（多文件结构：abstract.tex, introduction.tex, method.tex, experiments.tex, conclusion.tex, limitations.tex, ethics.tex, appendix.tex, references.bib）
- 目标 venue: ACL ARR May 2026 → EMNLP 2026 commitment
- 截止日期: 2026-05-25

## Venue 要求
- ACL ARR 长文: ≤8 页正文 + unlimited references
- Limitations section 必需（不计页数），位于 references 前
- ACL 模板 + [review] 选项，double-blind
- Responsible NLP Research Checklist **必需**（本文目前缺失）
- EMNLP 2026 评分标准: 5=Award (~2.5%), 4=Conference, 3=Findings, 2=Resubmit, 1=Do not resubmit

## 你的审查侧重

### 1. 新颖性评估
- 核心方法 STA = LOO ablation on solver inputs → ISR 指标。LOO ablation 本身是 standard（mutation testing, DeMillo 1978）
- 论文自称贡献在于 applying LOO as GRPO-hacking diagnostic + empirical phenomena
- Goodhart collapse 在 ISR 下是从定义可预测的（H7 tautologies 天然 ISR≥0.5）
- 论文 reframe 为 "empirical characterization of a predictable failure"
- 评估：这个 novelty 定位是否足以支撑一篇 EMNLP 长文？empirical phenomena（stochastic bifurcation, non-monotonic dose-response, nine hacking modes）是否有独立价值？

### 2. 写作质量
- 检查 AI 写作痕迹：em-dash (—), en-dash (–), -ing phrase endings, 过度分段, 黑名单词汇 (leverage, utilize, delve, myriad, plethora, paradigm shift, novel 过度使用)
- Abstract 信息密度：目前约 170 words，包含多少数字/统计量？是否像实验报告？
- Introduction 叙事流畅性：是否使用过多 \paragraph{} 导致碎片化？数字是否过载？
- 术语一致性：'non-hacking' vs 'genuine' vs 'structurally sound' 是否统一？
- 标题 "Valid but Vacuous" 中 'valid' 歧义（solver-verified vs logically valid）
- Contributions 列表（C1-C3）是否清晰无重叠
- Discussion/Conclusion 是否过于简短

### 3. 格式合规
- 页数：正文是否 ≤8 页
- \documentclass[11pt]{article} + \usepackage[review]{acl} ✓
- Anonymous: "Anonymous ACL submission" ✓
- Limitations section 位置：main.tex 中在 \bibliography 之前 ✓
- Ethics Statement 存在 ✓
- **Responsible NLP Checklist**：main.tex 中未包含——这是 ARR 硬性要求
- 引用格式：natbib (acl.sty 自带)
- 图表质量：Figure 1-5, Table 1-2 在正文 + 多个 Appendix tables

### 4. Related Work 完整性
- 当前分类：Formal reasoning with LLMs / Process rewards and reward shaping / Reward hacking and Goodhart's Law / Mutation testing
- references.bib 共 ~35 条引用
- 检查是否覆盖：直接相关方法、baseline 论文、评测 benchmark、理论基础
- 是否有重要遗漏（特别是 2025-2026 年的最新工作）
- 与 Kim et al. (2026) 的差异化是否充分
- 与 Mellgren et al. (2025), Logic-RL (2025) 的 positioning

### 5. 标题-实验范围匹配
- 标题 "RL-Trained Formal Reasoning" 但实验仅用 Qwen3 + FOLIO
- 是否应进一步收窄？

请按 ARR reviewer 标准出具完整审稿报告：Summary → Strengths → Weaknesses（按 critical/major/minor 分级）→ Questions → Missing References → Overall Assessment（含评分和置信度）→ Detailed Comments。

_source line 878 · 2026-05-18T10:02:24.923306+00:00_

1	\section{Introduction}
2	\label{sec:introduction}
3	
4	GRPO-trained formalizers converge to high prove rates on first-order logic problems ($97{\pm}2\%$ across three seeds, 1000 steps), yet at convergence produce effectively zero non-hacking outputs.
5	Each seed develops a distinct degenerate shortcut, all invisible to execution-based evaluation, which registers success regardless of whether the proof reflects faithful derivation.
6	Prove rate, the dominant evaluation metric for solver-backed formalizers~\citep{logic-lm, linc, satlm}, conflates faithful reasoning with degenerate shortcuts, a gap especially consequential under RL, where the optimizer exploits any shortcut the reward fails to penalize~\citep{skalse-reward-hacking, reward-hacking-survey}.
7	Recent work applies GRPO~\citep{deepseekmath} to train LLMs that generate solver-verified formal programs~\citep{mellgren-prolog-grpo, logic-rl} using binary execution feedback; this drives rapid prove-rate gains but provides no signal on structural quality.
8	Process reward models~\citep{lightman-verify-step, math-shepherd} require expensive annotations and do not transfer to arbitrary solver-backed settings.
9	A separate line of work documents that LLMs already game logical formalization at inference time~\citep{kim-game-formalization, bondarenko-spec-gaming}; what is missing is an annotation-free \emph{diagnostic} for structural integrity in solver-backed formal outputs.
10	
11	We address this gap with \emph{Solver Trace Ablation} (STA), which applies \emph{component necessity testing} (leave-one-out (LOO) ablation on solver inputs) to quantify structural integrity (Figure~\ref{fig:sta-overview}).
12	For each proved formalization, STA removes each component individually and re-runs the solver; the fraction of components whose removal causes proof failure yields the \emph{Irreducible Solver Rate} (ISR), with $\text{ISR}{=}1$ indicating full necessity and $\text{ISR}{=}0$ complete redundancy.
13	LOO ablation itse

### Tool result

_source line 879 · 2026-05-18T10:02:24.923591+00:00_

1	\begin{abstract}
2	GRPO-trained formalizers converge to high prove rates on FOLIO ($97{\pm}2\%$) yet produce effectively zero non-hacking outputs at convergence, a failure invisible to execution-based evaluation.
3	We propose \emph{Solver Trace Ablation} (STA), a leave-one-out necessity test on solver inputs whose derived metric, Irreducible Solver Rate (ISR), exposes a 99.5\% structural collapse hidden behind rising prove rates; a supervised baseline largely avoids hacking on identical data, a pattern consistent with RL-specific failure at convergent prove rates.
4	Repurposed as a reward signal, ISR exhibits Goodhart collapse at all four tested doses (trajectory-confirmed at $\beta{=}0.1$): tautological proofs receive artificially high ISR by construction, and any transient improvement over random controls disappears under extended training and stricter classification.
5	Across multiple reward conditions, logic types, and model scales, we catalog nine hacking modes and document that ISR is reliable as a diagnostic but unreliable as an optimization target, with collapse dynamics that resist prediction from the metric definition alone.\footnote{Code and data: \url{[anonymized]}}
6	\end{abstract}
7

1	\section{Related Work}
2	\label{sec:related-work}
3	
4	\paragraph{Formal reasoning with LLMs.}
5	LLM-solver pipelines~\citep{satlm, logic-lm, linc, fover-pei2025, lorp} and RL methods trained on solver feedback~\citep{polu2020generative, mellgren-prolog-grpo, logic-rl, deepseek-prover-v15, deepseek-prover-v2, deepseek-r1-2025} evaluate execution accuracy alone, with no diagnostic for structural quality.
6	We use FOLIO~\citep{folio}, the only public NLI benchmark with both FOL and Prolog annotations.
7	
8	\paragraph{Process rewards and reward shaping.}
9	Learned PRMs~\citep{lightman-verify-step, math-shepherd} provide step-level feedback but suffer from reward-model noise and distribution shift~\citep{processbench}; recent work extends PRMs to multi-domain settings~\citep{zeng2025versaprm} and process-level reasoning~\citep{khalifa2025thinkprm}. \citet{uesato2022solving} and \citet{gao-effective-rl-reward} show that auxiliary process/outcome rewards can \emph{hurt} RL training relative to success-only reward, a finding our ISR-as-reward results corroborate.
10	Unlike potential-based reward shaping~\citep{ng1999policy}, ISR augmentation does not preserve optimal policy, as it rewards output properties (component necessity) rather than state transitions, explaining the observed Goodhart vulnerability.
11	Symbolic feedback approaches~\citep{rlsf} use solver certificates as token-level rewards, achieving gains on program synthesis; ISR differs in operating at the component level (LOO ablation) rather than the token level, and our results show this structural signal is gamed when optimized.
12	Rubric-grounded GRPO~\citep{rubric-grpo} uses structured LLM-judge rewards to improve generalization; ISR is solver-derived and deterministic rather than judge-based, but both represent structured alternatives to scalar execution reward.
13	The shared lesson across these approaches is that reward augmentation introduces proxy risk; our contribution is providing an empirical instan
…[truncated 2608 chars]

1	\section{Method}
2	\label{sec:method}
3	
4	\begin{figure*}[t]
5	\centering
6	\includegraphics[width=0.92\textwidth]{figures/fig1_sta_overview.pdf}
7	\caption{Solver Trace Ablation (STA). (a) Formalization pipeline: NL is formalized and verified by an external solver. (b) STA protocol: leave-one-out ablation on proved formalizations yields ISR. (c) ISR as diagnostic vs.\ as reward: the diagnostic becomes the optimization target, exhibiting Goodhart effects.}
8	\label{fig:sta-overview}
9	\end{figure*}
10	
11	\subsection{Task and GRPO Training}
12	\label{sec:task}
13	
14	Given premises $P_1,\ldots,P_k$ and conclusion $C$, the task is to produce a formal program $F=\{c_1,\ldots,c_n\}$ verified by an external solver $S\in\{\text{Prover9, SWI-Prolog}\}$ (Figure~\ref{fig:sta-overview}a).
15	We train with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)~\citep{deepseekmath} ($K{=}4$), where each rollout receives binary execution reward $r_{\text{exec}}(x) = \mathbf{1}[S(F_x){=}\textit{proved}]$.
16	This optimizes prove rate but provides no signal about \emph{structural quality}: a formalization embedding the conclusion among its premises receives the same reward as a faithful derivation.
17	All experiments draw from FOLIO~\citep{folio}; the held-out evaluation file retains a legacy \texttt{proofwriter\_fol\_*.jsonl} filename (from ProofWriter~\citep{proofwriter}) but contains only FOLIO-derived examples.
18	
19	\subsection{Solver Trace Ablation (STA)}
20	\label{sec:sta}
21	
22	STA tests whether each component of a proved formalization is individually necessary for proof success (Figure~\ref{fig:sta-overview}b).
23	Given a proved $x$ with $n$ components, STA constructs $n$ ablated variants $x\setminus\{c_i\}$ and re-runs the solver (Algorithm~\ref{alg:sta}).
24	A component is \emph{necessary} if its removal causes proof failure.
25	What constitutes a component depends on the logic: premises and conclusion sub-expressions (quantifier, antecedent, consequent) in FOL, clauses or gro
…[truncated 6544 chars]

1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	We evaluate STA along three axes: ISR as a diagnostic for reward hacking (\S\ref{sec:diagnostic}); ISR as a training reward across four dose levels, where it exhibits Goodhart effects (\S\ref{sec:goodhart}); and STA as a cross-logic protocol (\S\ref{sec:crosslogic}).
5	Cross-scale evidence from Qwen3-1.7B reveals capacity-dependent Goodhart basins (\S\ref{sec:crossscale}).
6	A hacking taxonomy synthesizes the modes observed across conditions (\S\ref{sec:taxonomy}).
7	
8	\subsection{Setup}\label{sec:setup}
9	
10	We train Qwen3-4B~\citep{qwen3} with GRPO~\citep{deepseekmath} ($K{=}4$, lr $10^{-6}$, 500 steps default, 1000 where noted) over seeds $\{42,43,44\}$ on a fixed 500-example FOLIO~\citep{folio} subset held constant across all conditions; the SFT baseline is trained with negative log-likelihood (NLL) on gold formalizations.\footnote{All experiments use \texttt{data/train\_500\_seed42.jsonl}; seeds control initialization and rollout sampling only.}
11	Held-out evaluation uses 100 FOLIO examples (\texttt{proofwriter\_fol\_100.jsonl}) with greedy decoding (Appendix~\ref{app:setup}); training uses sampling ($T{=}0.7$), so evaluation reflects the mode of the learned distribution rather than the stochastic rollout behavior that drives RL updates.
12	We report prove rate (PR), proved-only ISR ($\text{ISR}^*$), hacking-mode rates from a two-stage classifier (strict text matching for H1/H7 plus functional analysis of the per-sample $n_{\text{necessary}}$ distribution; \S\ref{sec:taxonomy-method}), and a \emph{non-hacking} (N-H) rate: the residual of proved samples matching none of the nine cataloged patterns (\S\ref{sec:taxonomy}).

1	\section{Discussion and Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	
4	STA exposes structural degeneration invisible to execution-based evaluation (\S\ref{sec:diagnostic}), and ISR exhibits Goodhart collapse at all four tested doses (trajectory-confirmed at $\beta{=}0.1$; \S\ref{sec:goodhart}--\ref{sec:crossscale}).
5	At 500 steps, ISR produces descriptively more non-hacking output than random controls (pooled N-H: 23\% vs.\ 8\%); seed-level permutation tests are consistent in direction but underpowered ($p{=}0.26$, $n{=}5$), and under stricter classification the effect reverses ($\text{OR}{=}0.61$, n.s.).
6	Extended training confirms the window closes.
7	ISR is reliable as a diagnostic but unreliable as an optimizer: the same LOO bias that rewards genuine necessity also rewards tautological constructions.
8	While Goodhart collapse under LOO-based metrics is theoretically predictable, specific phenomena resist prediction from the metric definition alone: stochastic bifurcation (\S\ref{sec:goodhart}), non-monotonic dose-response across four $\beta$ levels, and cross-logic mode divergence where Prolog exhibits gaming pathways absent from FOL (\S\ref{sec:crosslogic}).
9	These details underscore the gap between predicting \emph{that} a metric will be gamed and predicting \emph{how}.
10	ISR and similar perturbation-based metrics should serve as post-hoc evaluation tools, not training signals.
11	
12	\paragraph{Scope.}
13	All experiments use a single model family (Qwen3-4B/1.7B), one dataset (FOLIO), and one RL algorithm (GRPO).
14	The specific hacking modes (H1, H7, H8) and their relative prevalence may differ under other architectures, training algorithms, or formal reasoning benchmarks; the structural vulnerability of LOO-based metrics to tautological exploits, however, follows from the metric definition and should transfer to any setting where the optimizer can reduce component count.
15	
16	\paragraph{Implications for reward design.}
17	Our results illustrate a concrete

1	\section{Limitations}
2	\label{sec:limitations}
3	
4	Experiments use a single model family (Qwen3-4B/1.7B) and a single structured-NLI dataset (FOLIO, 500 training examples); whether the specific hacking modes and collapse dynamics generalize to other architectures or larger-scale formal reasoning benchmarks remains an open question.
5	ISR quantifies syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness: a high-ISR proof can still be logically vacuous if its individually necessary premises are collectively inconsistent or semantically ungrounded.
6	A downstream NLI evaluation confirms this gap, finding ISR and task accuracy uncorrelated (Spearman $\rho{=}{-}0.049$, $p{=}0.187$; Appendix~\ref{app:extended-discussion}).
7	Five seeds demonstrate qualitative divergence but limit statistical power for seed-level permutation tests ($p{\geq}0.26$; minimum achievable $p{=}0.008$ at $n{=}5$). CMH tests yield $p{<}10^{-9}$ but assume within-stratum independence, which is violated when samples share a checkpoint and decoding strategy; CMH $p$-values are therefore inflated and should be interpreted as directional indicators, not calibrated significance levels.
8	STA requires solvers supporting component-level input manipulation.
9	Non-hacking is a residual classification; undiscovered gaming modes would reduce the true non-hacking rate, as illustrated by H7b (obfuscated tautology evading text-match classifiers) and query-as-fact gaming in Prolog.
10	
11	\paragraph{Evaluation as temporal snapshot.}
12	All 500-step results represent a temporal snapshot, not an equilibrium.
13	Trajectory analysis on seeds~42 and~45 (1000 steps) confirms that the ${\sim}23\%$ non-hacking window observed at 500 steps under ISR $\beta{=}0.1$ is transient (s42: $17\%{\to}2\%$; s45: $55\%{\to}0\%$ non-hacking).
14	Readers should interpret the 500-step non-hacking rates as upper bounds on long-run structural quality under continued training.
15

_source line 891 · 2026-05-18T10:02:33.464496+00:00_

1	\section{Ethics Statement}
2	\label{sec:ethics}
3	
4	This work documents failure modes of reward optimization in solver-backed RL systems.
5	Our findings (that structural diagnostics can be gamed when used as training signals) carry implications for AI safety: they demonstrate that metrics designed to detect misalignment may themselves become targets of optimization, a concern relevant to scalable oversight.
6	We release our taxonomy and diagnostic tools to help practitioners detect reward hacking in their own systems.
7	All experiments use publicly available datasets (FOLIO) and open-weight models (Qwen3-4B); no human subjects are involved.
8

# ACL Rolling Review Form - Evaluation Dimensions

## 1. **Reviewer Confidence** (1-5 Scale)
- **5**: "Positive that my evaluation is correct. I read the paper very carefully and am familiar with related work."
- **4**: "Quite sure. I tried to check the important points carefully."
- **3**: "Pretty sure, but there's a chance I missed something."
- **2**: "Willing to defend my evaluation, but it is fairly likely that I missed some details."
- **1**: "Not my area, or paper is very hard to understand."

## 2. **Soundness** (1-5 Scale)
- **5**: "Excellent: This study is one of the most thorough I have seen, given its type."
- **4**: "Strong: This study provides sufficient support for all of its claims."
- **3**: "Acceptable: This study provides sufficient support for its main claims."
- **2**: "Poor: Some of the main claims are not sufficiently supported."
- **1**: "Major Issues: This study is not yet sufficiently thorough to warrant publication."

## 3. **Excitement** (1-5 Scale)
- **5**: "Highly Exciting: I would recommend this paper to others."
- **4**: "Exciting: I would mention this paper to others."
- **3**: "Interesting: I might mention some points of this paper to others."
- **2**: "Potentially Interesting: this paper does not resonate with me."
- **1**: "Not Exciting: this paper does not resonate with me, and I don't think it would with others."

## 4. **Overall Assessment** (1-5 Scale)
- **5**: "Consider for Award"
- **4**: "Conference"
- **3**: "Findings"
- **2**: "Resubmit next cycle"
- **1**: "Do not resubmit"

## 5. **Reproducibility** (1-5 Scale)
- **5**: "They could easily reproduce the results."
- **4**: "They could mostly reproduce the results."
- **3**: "They could reproduce the results with some difficulty."
- **2**: "They would be hard pressed to reproduce the results."
- **1**: "They would not be able to reproduce the results."

{
  "text": "\n# Reviewer 2 — Round 1 审稿报告\n\n**论文**: \"Valid but Vacuous: Reward Hacking in RL-Trained Formal Reasoning\"\n**Venue**: ACL ARR May 2026 → EMNLP 2026\n**审查侧重**: 新颖性 + 写作质量 + 格式合规 + Related Work\n\n---\n\n## 总体评分\n\n**Overall**: 3.0 (Findings)\n**一句话总结**: 一篇实验扎实但方法新颖性有限的实证研究——LOO ablation 本身是标准技术，Goodhart collapse 由 ISR 定义可预测，核心贡献是特定 RL 设置下的经验性 failure-mode 编录，更适合 Findings 而非 Main Conference。\n\n---\n\n## 官方维度打分（ACL ARR Review Form）\n\n| 维度 | 分数 | 理由摘要 |\n|------|------|----------|\n| Soundness | 3.5/5 | 实验设计合理（多 seed、random control、SFT baseline、cross-logic/cross-scale），但唯一模型家族+单一数据集限制了 claim 泛化性；统计检验自承 underpowered (seed-level $p{=}0.26$) |\n| Excitement | 2.5/5 | 问题重要（formal reasoning 下的 reward hacking），但核心发现（Goodhart collapse under ISR）由 metric 定义本身可预测；nine-mode taxonomy 有实用价值但叙事上更像 experiment log |\n| Reproducibility | 4/5 | Hyperparameters 完整（Table A1），algorithm 清晰，承诺 code release，但 2/5 ISR seed checkpoints 已丢失 (s43/s44 无法 H8 audit) |\n| Overall Assessment | 3.0 | Findings 级别 |\n| Confidence | 4/5 | 熟悉 RL for reasoning 和 reward hacking 领域，仔细阅读了全文和附录 |\n\n---\n\n## Must-Fix 问题（影响接收决定）\n\n**N1** [critical] Responsible NLP Research Checklist 缺失——desk rejection 风险\n- 具体位置：main.tex 全文\n- 问题：ARR 自 2024 年 12 月起强制执行 Responsible NLP Research checklist。main.tex 中没有包含该 checklist。根据 ARR Authors Beware 页面：\"Starting from December 2024, ARR enforces desk rejections for incorrect, incomplete or misleading filing of the responsible NLP checklist.\"\n- 为什么影响接收：**不提交 = desk reject**，不会进入审稿流程\n- 建议修改方向：从 https://aclrollingreview.org/responsibleNLPresearch/ 下载 checklist，逐项认真填写，作为 appendix 附在论文末尾\n\n**N2** [critical] 方法新颖性不足以支撑 EMNLP 长文 Main Conference 定位\n- 具体位置：Section 2 (§2, line 13), Section 3.2 (§3.2, line 27), Abstract (line 3)\n- 问题：核心方法 STA = LOO ablation on solver inputs → ISR metric。论文自身多次承认 \"LOO ablation itself is standard\" (Introduction line 13, Method §3.2 line 27, Related Work §2 line 25)。ISR 的 Goodhart vulnerability 同样由定义可推导：single-premise-equals-conclusion 构造天然 ISR≥0.5 (Method §3.2 line 58)。论文的 reframe——\"the specific dynamics provide actionable diagnostics not derivable from the general principle\" (Introduction line 17)——本质是说：我们用已知工具在特定场景下记录了预期中的失败。这属于 application + empirical catalog，而非 methodological contribution。\n  - C1 (ISR as diagnostic): 是 LOO 在特定领域的直接应用\n  - C2 (Goodhart dynamics): stochastic bifurcation 和 non-monotonic dose-response 是有趣的经验观察，但论文自己的分析已解释了机制 (H7 作为 absorbing state, §4.3 line 89)\n  - C3 (cross-logic): Prolog 3 seeds 全部 collapse，确认了已知 vulnerability\n- 为什么影响接收：EMNLP Main Conference (Overall 4.0) 通常期望 methodological or conceptual advance，而非 \"applying standard technique to new domain + documenting expected failure\"。当前定位更适合 Findings。\n- 建议修改方向：(a) 强化 nine-mode taxonomy 作为独立贡献——目前散落在多个 section，未作为 framework 系统提出；(b) 增加至少一个非 trivial 的 mitigation 实验（如 ISR as post-hoc filter 或 premise-count-normalized ISR），使论文从 \"diagnosis\" 走向 \"diagnosis + partial solution\"；(c) 考虑收窄为 short paper 并聚焦 diagnostic contribution\n\n**N3** [critical] 标题暗示的范围远超实验覆盖\n- 具体位置：标题 \"RL-Trained Formal Reasoning\"；实验仅 Section 4 (Qwen3-4B/1.7B + FOLIO + GRPO)\n- 问题：\"RL-Trained Formal Reasoning\" 暗示对 RL + formal reasoning 的通用结论，但实验仅涉及 1 个模型家族 (Qwen3)、1 个数据集 (FOLIO, 500 训练样本)、1 个 RL 算法 (GRPO)。Discussion §5 的 Scope 段 (line 13) 承认了这一点，但标题与 claim 之间的 gap 仍然过大。\n- 为什么影响接收：审稿人和读者会期望至少涵盖 >1 model family 或 >1 benchmark（如 ProofWriter、PrOntoQA、或数学推理 benchmark）。单一设置下的 findings 用广泛标题包装会被视为 overclaiming。\n- 建议修改方向：将标题收窄，如 \"Valid but Vacuous: Reward Hacking in GRPO-Trained FOLIO Formalizers\" 或 \"...in Solver-Backed RL on First-Order Logic\"\n\n---\n\n## Should-Fix 问题（影响评分但不一票否决）\n\n**W1** [major] Abstract 和 Introduction 数字过载，读起来像实验报告而非有说服力的故事\n- 具体位置：Abstract (全部 5 行), Introduction (lines 4-27)\n- 问题：\n  - Abstract 在 ~170 words 内塞入了 $97{\\pm}2\\%$、99.5\\%、ISR≥0.5、$\\beta{=}0.1$ 等至少 7 个具体数字/统计量，以及 \"nine hacking modes\"、\"four tested doses\" 等枚举。审稿人 30 秒内无法消化。\n  - Introduction 更甚：line 4 就给出 \"$97{\\pm}2\\%$ across three seeds, 1000 steps\"，line 14 \"ISR$^*$: 0.642→0.003\"，line 15 \"PR=13.0% with 92.3% non-hacking\"。这些数字在 Introduction 里过早出现，打断了叙事流。\n- 建议：Abstract 保留 1-2 个 headline 数字，其余用定性描述（如 \"near-total structural collapse\"）替代。Introduction 前 3 段讲故事（问题→gap→insight），数字推迟到 Contributions 列表或引用 Section。\n\n**W2** [major] Discussion + Conclusion 合并且过短\n- 具体位置：Section 5 (conclusion.tex, 21 行正文含 3 个 \\paragraph{} header)\n- 问题：一个 EMNLP 长文的 Discussion 应至少讨论：(1) findings 对 reward design 的具体启示（超出 \"keep diagnostic decoupled from training\"），(2) 与 concurrent work 的 positioning（Spurious Rewards, METR report），(3) failure mode 的可推广性到 math reasoning 等其他 formal domains。当前 Discussion 基本是结果复述 + 两段 generic implications。\n- 建议：扩展 Discussion 为独立 section（与 Conclusion 分开），增加对 (a) ISR 作为 post-hoc filter 的可行性分析，(b) premise-count normalization 方案的讨论，(c) 与 concurrent reward-hacking detection work 的对比\n\n**W3** [major] SFT vs RL 比较受 prove rate 严重 confound\n- 具体位置：Section 4.2 (experiments.tex lines 37-40)\n- 问题：SFT PR=13% vs RL PR=97%。论文承认 \"fully PR-matched comparison between RL and SFT is unavailable\" (Introduction line 15)。100-step checkpoint (PR=32%) 只是 partial control。在 PR 差距如此大的情况下，claim \"hacking being RL-specific\" (Introduction line 15, experiments.tex line 38 \"consistent with the failure being RL-specific\") 过强。SFT 可能仅仅因为 PR 太低而缺乏 hacking 的机会。\n- 建议：(a) 弱化措辞为 \"SFT avoids hacking at observed PR levels, but the PR confound prevents causal attribution\"；(b) 或者增加 SFT with more training data/epochs 使 PR 接近 RL 水平的实验\n\n**W4** [major] 统计效力不足且核心 claim 依赖 pooled tests\n- 具体位置：experiments.tex lines 93-96; limitations.tex lines 7-8; appendix.tex lines 117-122\n- 问题：论文正确地识别了 within-seed dependence 问题（共享 checkpoint + greedy decoding 导致样本非独立），但 seed-level permutation tests 只有 n=5 ($p{=}0.26$, minimum achievable $p{=}0.008$)。pooled Fisher exact tests 和 CMH tests 的 p-values 被作者自己标记为 \"inflated\"。然而 Table 1 的 pooled non-hacking rates (23% vs 8%) 被用于支撑 narrative，且 abstract 中的 claim 没有附加这些 caveats。\n- 建议：在 Abstract 和 main text 的关键 claim 处明确标注统计不确定性，避免给读者 \"ISR > random\" 的确定性印象\n\n**W5** [warning] \"non-hacking\" 残差分类的可靠性问题\n- 具体位置：experiments.tex line 12; limitations.tex lines 9, 31-33\n- 问题：Non-hacking 是通过排除 9 种已知模式后的残差定义的。论文自己发现了 H7b (obfuscated tautology, 14 samples evasion) 和 query-as-fact gaming (Prolog seed 44, 95/97 false negatives)。这意味着当前的 non-hacking rate 几乎确定是高估的。当论文说 \"23% non-hacking\" 时，真实值可能显著更低。\n- 建议：在所有报告 non-hacking rate 的地方加注 \"upper bound\"（部分已做，如 Table 1 的 $^*$ 标记，但不够一致）\n\n**W6** [warning] \\paragraph{} 过度使用导致正文碎片化\n- 具体位置：method.tex (7 个), conclusion.tex (2 个), limitations.tex (7 个), introduction.tex (1 个)\n- 问题：Method §3.2 在约 50 行文本中使用了 5 个 \\paragraph{}，平均每 10 行一个。这将连续论证切割成片段，削弱了阅读流畅性。Discussion/Conclusion 用 \\paragraph{Scope.} 和 \\paragraph{Implications...} 把本就很短的 section 进一步分割。Limitations 的 7 个 \\paragraph{} 使其看起来像 bullet list 而非论述。\n- 建议：合并相关 \\paragraph{} 为连续段落；Method 中 ISR limitations, premise-count confound, LOO vs Shapley 可以合并为一个 \"Limitations and Design Choices\" 子节\n\n---\n\n## 小问题（不影响接收）\n\n- **M1** 标题 \"Valid\" 歧义：solver-verified (本文含义) vs logically valid (形式逻辑含义)。建议括号注释或换词（如 \"Verified but Vacuous\"）\n- **M2** appendix.tex line 1: 注释中有 em-dash (—)：\"% Appendix — solver-trace-ablation\"。虽在注释中不影响输出，但建议统一清除\n- **M3** FOLIO bib 年份可能有误：verify_references 显示 bib 中 year=2024 vs OpenAlex year=2022（FOLIO 最初 2022 发表于 arXiv，2024 在 EMNLP）。需确认使用的是哪个版本\n- **M4** experiments.tex line 17: 遗留文件名 \"proofwriter_fol_100.jsonl\" 虽有解释 (method.tex line 17, appendix line 54)，但在三处分别解释同一件事，建议统一到一处\n- **M5** 术语：论文主要使用 \"non-hacking\"，但偶见 \"genuine\" (无) 和 \"structural novelty\" (experiments.tex line 13)。总体一致性尚可。\n- **M6** \"Strict non-hacking\" 在 §4.5 引入但在 §4.2-4.4 未使用，跨 section 定义不一致\n\n---\n\n## Missing References（可能遗漏的重要相关工作）\n\n1. **\"Detecting Proxy Gaming in RL and LLM Alignment via Evaluator Stress Tests\"** (2025, arXiv 2507.05619) — 提出 Evaluator Stress Test (EST) 检测 proxy gaming，与本文的 \"diagnostic becomes gamed\" 主题直接相关，应在 §2 Reward hacking 段讨论\n2. **\"Pushing the Boundaries of Natural Reasoning: Interleaved Bonus from Formal-Logic Verification\"** (2025, arXiv 2601.22642) — 使用 formal logic verification 作为 interleaved bonus 引导 reasoning，是 ISR-as-reward 的替代方案\n3. **\"Reward Hacking as Equilibrium under Finite Evaluation\"** (2026, arXiv 2603.28063) — 将 reward hacking 形式化为有限评估下的均衡，提供了比 Manheim taxonomy 更 formal 的理论框架\n4. **\"Is It Thinking or Cheating? Detecting Implicit Reward Hacking by Measuring Reasoning Effort\"** (2025, arXiv 2510.01367) — 通过测量 reasoning effort 检测 implicit reward hacking，与 STA 的 structural diagnostic 形成对照\n\n当前 46 条引用的覆盖面总体合理，上述遗漏不构成 critical issue，但补充后会加强 positioning。\n\n---\n\n## Related Work 评估\n\n**覆盖度**: 4 个子节（Formal reasoning, Process rewards, Reward hacking/Goodhart, Mutation testing）覆盖了主要方向。Kim et al. (2026) 的差异化清晰（inference-time vs training-time gaming, §2 line 20）。Mellgren et al. (2025) 和 Logic-RL (2025) 作为 direct related work 有引用。\n\n**不足**:\n- 缺少 2025-2026 年最新的 reward hacking detection/mitigation 工作（见上方 missing references）\n- Mutation testing 子节过短（2 行），与 Related Work 其他子节不成比例\n- 与 \"Spurious Rewards\" (Shao et al., 2025) 的对比只是一句 \"documents spurious reward signals in RLVR\" (§2 line 18)，没有具体说明 ISR 与 spurious reward 概念的异同\n\n**结构**: 每个子节最后都尝试 position 自己（\"our contribution is...\"），这是好的。但 §2 Reward hacking 段过长（7 行密集引用列表），可以精简。\n\n---\n\n## 主要优势\n\n1. **实验设计的严谨性和透明度**：多 seed (3-5)、random control、SFT baseline、cross-logic (FOL+Prolog)、cross-scale (4B+1.7B)、trajectory analysis (500→1000 steps)。对 premise-count confound、statistical power limitations、checkpoint loss 的诚实报告在 ML 论文中难能可贵。\n2. **Nine-mode hacking taxonomy 的实用价值**：H7b (obfuscated tautology) 和 query-as-fact gaming 的发现展示了 gaming 策略的创造性和 classifier evasion 能力，对构建更鲁棒的 hacking detector 有直接启发。\n3. **\"Diagnostic vs optimizer\" 框架**：将 ISR 的双重角色（观察者 vs 优化目标）清晰框架化，对 reward design 领域有实际指导意义。\n4. **AI 写作痕迹极少**：正文无 em-dash，无解释性 en-dash，无 \"leverage/utilize/delve\" 等 AI 标志词汇，专业写作水准。\n5. **Limitations section 详尽且诚实**：7 个子节覆盖了几乎所有可预见的批评点，未试图隐藏弱点。\n\n---\n\n## 总结评分理由\n\n这是一篇实验上扎实、报告上诚实的 empirical study，但核心方法贡献（LOO ablation → ISR）是已有技术的直接应用，主要发现（Goodhart collapse）由 metric 定义本身可预测。论文的真正贡献在于经验性编录（nine hacking modes, stochastic bifurcation, dose-response dynamics），这在 Findings track 有其价值，但不足以支撑 Main Conference 长文。格式上，Responsible NLP Checklist 的缺失是 desk reject 级别的问题，必须立即修复。标题应收窄以匹配实验范围。\n",

}

(2025) 和 Logic-RL (2025) 作为 direct related work 有引用。\n\n**不足**:\n- 缺少 2025-2026 年最新的 reward hacking detection/mitigation 工作（见上方 missing references）\n- Mutation testing 子节过短（2 行），与 Related Work 其他子节不成比例\n- 与 \"Spurious Rewards\" (Shao et al., 2025) 的对比只是一句 \"documents spurious reward signals in RLVR\" (§2 line 18)，没有具体说明 ISR 与 spurious reward 概念的异同\n\n**结构**: 每个子节最后都尝试 position 自己（\"our contribution is...\"），这是好的。但 §2 Reward hacking 段过长（7 行密集引用列表），可以精简。\n\n---\n\n## 主要优势\n\n1. **实验设计的严谨性和透明度**：多 seed (3-5)、random control、SFT baseline、cross-logic (FOL+Prolog)、cross-scale (4B+1.7B)、trajectory analysis (500→1000 steps)。对 premise-count confound、statistical power limitations、checkpoint loss 的诚实报告在 ML 论文中难能可贵。\n2. **Nine-mode hacking taxonomy 的实用价值**：H7b (obfuscated tautology) 和 query-as-fact gaming 的发现展示了 gaming 策略的创造性和 classifier evasion 能力，对构建更鲁棒的 hacking detector 有直接启发。\n3. **\"Diagnostic vs optimizer\" 框架**：将 ISR 的双重角色（观察者 vs 优化目标）清晰框架化，对 reward design 领域有实际指导意义。\n4. **AI 写作痕迹极少**：正文无 em-dash，无解释性 en-dash，无 \"leverage/utilize/delve\" 等 AI 标志词汇，专业写作水准。\n5. **Limitations section 详尽且诚实**：7 个子节覆盖了几乎所有可预见的批评点，未试图隐藏弱点。\n\n---\n\n## 总结评分理由\n\n这是一篇实验上扎实、报告上诚实的 empirical study，但核心方法贡献（LOO ablation → ISR）是已有技术的直接应用，主要发现（Goodhart collapse）由 metric 定义本身可预测。论文的真正贡献在于经验性编录（nine hacking modes, stochastic bifurcation, dose-response dynamics），这在 Findings track 有其价值，但不足以支撑 Main Conference 长文。格式上，Responsible NLP Checklist 的缺失是 desk reject 级别的问题，必须立即修复。标题应收窄以匹配实验范围。\n",

  }
}

# Reviewer 1 — Round 1 审稿报告

**审查侧重**: 方法严谨性 + 实验设计 + 统计分析

## 总体评分
**Overall**: 3.0 / 5 (Findings)

| 维度 | 分数 |
|------|------|
| Soundness | 2.5/5 |
| Excitement | 3.5/5 |
| Reproducibility | 4/5 |
| Confidence | 4/5 |

## Must-Fix 问题

**N1 [critical]** 统计检验力根本不足以支撑核心动态学 claims
- 主实验 3 seeds，仅 β=0.1 扩展到 5 seeds。n=3 permutation test 最小 p=0.05，无法拒绝零假设。"Stochastic bifurcation"（3 seeds 分化为 3 种 failure modes）在 n=3 时与随机变异不可区分。"Non-monotonic dose-response" 基于 4 β × 3 seeds。
- 建议: 增加 seeds ≥10；将语言从 "stochastic bifurcation" 降级为 "preliminary patterns"；对 pooled tests 应用 BH 校正。

**N2 [critical]** ISR premise-count confound 与 Goodhart 诊断存在循环性
- ISR ≥ 0.5 当 n ≤ 2 by construction。RL 倾向更短 formalization → ISR 机械性升高。Table 4 校正后 OR 从 3.48 降至 0.61（方向反转），说明大部分 Goodhart 效应可能仅是 premise-count confound。
- 建议: 引入 normalized ISR；在主文突出报告 premise-count-corrected OR。

**N3 [critical]** SFT baseline PR 悬殊使 "RL-specific" claim 不可信
- SFT PR=13% vs exec-only PR=93%+。13 个 proved samples 中 12 个 non-hacking 可能是 sampling bias。100-step RL checkpoint 控制引入训练阶段 confound。
- 建议: 提供 PR-matched 对照。

## Should-Fix 问题

**W1 [major]** FOLIO 单数据集 + Prolog 同数据集伪独立验证
**W2 [major]** Hacking taxonomy 仅验证 H1/H7 precision（50 samples），其余 modes 未验证
**W3 [major]** 多重比较未校正
**W4 [moderate]** Cross-scale 1.7B 单 seed 结果作为 contribution 支撑不足
**W5 [moderate]** O(1) binary ablation ISR*≈0.007 低于 exec-only 的 0.132，反直觉结果未解释

## 小问题
- Responsible NLP Research Checklist 缺失
- Figure 2 线条重叠
- "stochastic bifurcation" 术语误导
- 计算开销未讨论
- FOLIO bib 年份

## 优势
1. 问题定位精准
2. STA/ISR 概念简洁
3. 九种 hacking mode qualitative 分析有价值
4. 诚实的 self-criticism
5. Prolog cross-logic extension 提供初步 generalizability

1	\section{Introduction}
2	\label{sec:introduction}
3	
4	GRPO-trained formalizers converge to high prove rates on first-order logic problems ($97{\pm}2\%$ across three seeds, 1000 steps), yet at convergence produce effectively zero non-hacking outputs.
5	Each seed develops a distinct degenerate shortcut, all invisible to execution-based evaluation, which registers success regardless of whether the proof reflects faithful derivation.
6	Prove rate, the dominant evaluation metric for solver-backed formalizers~\citep{logic-lm, linc, satlm}, conflates faithful reasoning with degenerate shortcuts, a gap especially consequential under RL, where the optimizer exploits any shortcut the reward fails to penalize~\citep{skalse-reward-hacking, reward-hacking-survey}.
7	Recent work applies GRPO~\citep{deepseekmath} to train LLMs that generate solver-verified formal programs~\citep{mellgren-prolog-grpo, logic-rl} using binary execution feedback; this drives rapid prove-rate gains but provides no signal on structural quality.
8	Process reward models~\citep{lightman-verify-step, math-shepherd} require expensive annotations and do not transfer to arbitrary solver-backed settings.
9	A separate line of work documents that LLMs already game logical formalization at inference time~\citep{kim-game-formalization, bondarenko-spec-gaming}; what is missing is an annotation-free \emph{diagnostic} for structural integrity in solver-backed formal outputs.
10	
11	We address this gap with \emph{Solver Trace Ablation} (STA), which applies \emph{component necessity testing} (leave-one-out (LOO) ablation on solver inputs) to quantify structural integrity (Figure~\ref{fig:sta-overview}).
12	For each proved formalization, STA removes each component individually and re-runs the solver; the fraction of components whose removal causes proof failure yields the \emph{Irreducible Solver Rate} (ISR), with $\text{ISR}{=}1$ indicating full necessity and $\text{ISR}{=}0$ complete redundancy.

inference。\n\n---\n\n### R1-N2: ISR premise-count confound 与 Goodhart 诊断的循环性\n\n**[REBUT]** R1 的 framing 有误：premise-count confound 不是 Goodhart 效应的 competing explanation，而是 Goodhart 效应的 mechanism。\n\nR1 说 \"Table 4 校正后 OR 从 3.48 降至 0.61（方向反转），说明大部分 Goodhart 效应可能仅是 premise-count confound\"。这混淆了两个不同的比较：\n- **Table A4 (appendix line 90-114) 的 OR=3.48 vs 0.61**：这是 ISR vs Random 的 **non-hacking rate** 对比，不是 Goodhart 效应本身。5-seed pooled 23% vs 8% (OR=3.48) → 3-seed H8+FN corrected 6% vs 9.5% (OR=0.61)。方向反转说明的是 \"ISR 是否比 random 产生更多 non-hacking output\" 这个 claim 不可靠，论文已明确承认 (experiments.tex lines 93-96, 98-99)。\n- **Goodhart 效应本身**：指的是 H7 tautology 在 ISR reward 下的 dominance。H7 tautology (single premise = conclusion) 在所有 4 个 β 下出现，ISR β=0.2 时 3/3 seeds 100% H7 (Table A3, appendix line 159-175)。这不是 premise-count confound 的 artifact——是模型 **主动** 产生 n=2 的 tautological formalization 以最大化 ISR reward。Confound 是说 \"已有的短 formalization 会被 ISR 高估\"；Goodhart 是说 \"模型学会了产生短的 tautological formalization 来 exploit ISR\"。前者是观察偏差，后者是行为变化。\n\n论文在 Method §3.2 lines 56-62 已经区分了这两种情况：\"The confound manifests in two distinct scenarios: as a source of Goodhart vulnerability when ISR is optimized (H7 tautologies exploit low n to inflate ISR), and as a selection effect when ISR is observed.\"\n\n但我同意 R1 的 **建议** 是合理的：normalized ISR 和 premise-count-corrected comparisons 应在主文更突出报告（目前 hacking; Appendix §A.9 line 672-676) 提供了部分控制，但 R1 正确指出这引入了 training-stage confound。\n\n补充 R1 的观点：论文还可以用 **SFT with data augmentation** 或 **continued SFT training** 来尝试提升 SFT PR，或者用 **rejection sampling on SFT** 来模拟更高 PR。当前的 SFT baseline 更像是 \"SFT doesn't learn to prove well\" 的发现，而不是 \"SFT doesn't hack\" 的证据。\n\n---\n\n## Should-Fix 逐条回应\n\n### R1-W1: FOLIO 单数据集 + Prolog 同数据集伪独立验证\n\n**[AGREE]** 部分同意。\n\nCross-logic extension 的价值在于 **不同 solver 语义下的 gaming pathway divergence**（Prolog 发现了 FOL 中不存在的 query-as-fact gaming, Table A7 appendix line 577-608），而不是作为独立数据集验证。但 R1 正确指出这不构成真正的 out-of-distribution generalization。论文应明确 Prolog 实验的 scope：\"tests whether gaming generalizes across logic formalisms on the same data\" 而非 \"cross-domain validation\"。\n\n### R1-W2: audit (Appendix line 241-253)，H9 和 query-as-fact 是 functional analysis 发现的。但 R1 的核心 concern 是合理的：没有 systematic recall estimation。论文自己发现了 H7b (14 samples evasion, Appendix line 255-261) 证明了 text-match seed\"。\n\nPer-seed breakdown 在 experiments.tex lines 131-135 有详细报告：ISR β=0.1 的 3 seeds 分别产生 H1 (s42)、H7a (s43)、mixed (s44)；Random β=0.1 的 s42 发现了 H9 (contradiction injection)。这是 3 seeds 的结果，不是单 seed。\n\n如果 R1 指的是某些 specific observations（如 H9 只在 1.7B random s42 出现），那确实是 single-seed observation，论文也标记为 \"isolated observation\" (experiments.tex line 135: \"H9 never appears at 4B scale\")。但整体 cross-scale contribution 是基于 9 runs 的。\n\n### R1-W5: O(1) binary ablation ISR*≈0.007 低于 exec-only 的 0.132\n\n**[REBUT]** 论文在 Appendix §A.9 (appendix lines 685-689) 已解释此结果。\n\nR1 [Round 1 审稿请求]:
你是 Reviewer 2，负责审查新颖性/贡献评估、Related Work 完整性、写作质量和格式合规。

## 论文信息
- 标题: "Valid but Vacuous: Reward Hacking in GRPO-Trained FOL Reasoners"
- 路径: `projects/solver-trace-ablation/paper/main.tex`（各 section 为独立 .tex 文件在同一目录下）
- Venue: EMNLP 2026（通过 ARR May 2026 cycle 提交）
- 截止日期: 2026-05-25
- 类型: Long paper
- 页数限制: 8 页正文，unlimited limitations/ethics/references/appendix
- 模板: ACL style with `[review]` option
- 匿名: Double-blind
- EMNLP 2026 评分标准: 5=Award, 4=Conference, 3=Findings, 2=Resubmit, 1=Do not resubmit

## 你的审查侧重

### 1. 新颖性与贡献评估
- STA/ISR 本身是 LOO ablation 的直接应用（论文也承认），方法新颖性如何？
- Goodhart 效应在理论上是可预测的（论文也承认），实验发现的增量价值在哪？
- 三个 contribution（诊断、Goodhart dynamics、cross-logic）的份量是否够 EMNLP Findings？
- 论文定位为"case study"/"cautionary results"——这个定位对于 EMNLP 是否合适？
- 与 Kim et al. (2026) "Do LLMs Game Formalization?" 的区分是否充分？

### 2. Related Work 完整性
- 引用覆盖度：neurosymbolic reasoning、reward hacking、process reward models、mutation testing 四个方向是否充分？
- 是否有重要遗漏的相关工作？（特别是：formal verification RL、reward shaping、RL alignment 领域）
- Related Work 是否做到了 positioning 而非 survey？
- 与最新 2025-2026 论文的对比是否及时？

### 3. 写作质量（严格检查 AI 写作痕迹）
- **Em-dash / En-dash 检查**：全文是否有解释性 dash？
- **-ing 短语结尾**检查：是否有典型 AI 写作模式？
- **过度分段**：段落长度是否自然？
- **过渡词**使用是否过度？（Furthermore/Moreover/Additionally 连续使用？）
- **用词黑名单**：delve/leverage/utilize/facilitate/myriad/plethora 等？
- **叙事流畅性**：Abstract → Intro → Method → Experiments → Discussion 的主线是否统一？
- **专有名词定义**：首次出现的术语是否都有定义？

### 4. 格式合规性
- 正文页数是否 ≤ 8 页？（Discussion/Conclusion 结束的位置）
- Limitations 是否为独立 section（不计入页数）？
- Responsible NLP Checklist 是否完整、诚实？
- 匿名化是否彻底？（检查 URL、自引、致谢）
- 图表格式：字体大小 ≥ 7pt？caption 自包含？数值精度统一？
- 引用格式是否符合 ACL natbib 标准？
- Appendix 是否为双栏格式？

### 5. 图表评估
- Figure 1（STA overview）：信息密度是否足够？概念图是否 trivial？
- Figure 2（ISR collapse）：是否清晰传达核心 finding？
- Figure 3（hacking composition）：信息编码是否合理？
- Table 1-3：数据呈现是否高效？小数精度是否统一？最优值是否标注？

请按照标准审稿格式输出：Summary → Strengths → Weaknesses → Questions → Missing References → Overall Assessment（含评分和置信度）→ Detailed Comments。

# ACL Rolling Review Form Dimensions

## 1. **Soundness** (1-5 scale)
- 5: "Excellent: This study is one of the most thorough I have seen"
- 4: "Strong: This study provides sufficient support for all claims"
- 3: "Acceptable: This study provides sufficient support for main claims"
- 2: "Poor: Some main claims are not sufficiently supported"
- 1: "Major Issues: This study is not sufficiently thorough to warrant publication"

## 2. **Excitement** (1-5 scale)
- 5: "Highly Exciting: I would recommend this paper to others"
- 4: "Exciting: I would mention this paper to others"
- 3: "Interesting: I might mention some points to others"
- 2: "Potentially Interesting: does not resonate with me"
- 1: "Not Exciting: does not resonate with me"

## 3. **Overall Assessment** (1-5 scale)
- 5: Consider for Award (top 2.5% papers)
- 4: Conference acceptance
- 3: Findings track acceptance
- 2: Resubmit next cycle
- 1: Do not resubmit

## 4. **Reproducibility** (1-5 scale)
- 5: "They could easily reproduce the results"
- 4: "They could mostly reproduce the results"
- 3: "They could reproduce with some difficulty"
- 2: "They would be hard pressed to reproduce"
- 1: "They would not be able to reproduce"

## 5. **Datasets & Software** (1-5 scales)
Both use identical scales: Enabling (5), Useful (4), Potentially useful (3), Documentary (2), None (1)

_source line 971 · 2026-05-18T11:23:22.505505+00:00_

1	\begin{abstract}
2	RL-trained formalizers achieve high prove rates on logical reasoning tasks, yet these gains mask structural degeneration invisible to execution-based evaluation.
3	We propose \emph{Solver Trace Ablation} (STA), a leave-one-out necessity test on solver inputs that quantifies structural integrity through the \emph{Irreducible Solver Rate} (ISR).
4	Applied as a post-hoc diagnostic, ISR reveals a $99.5\%$ structural collapse hidden behind rising prove rates; a supervised baseline on identical data largely avoids this failure, consistent with it being RL-specific.
5	Repurposed as a reward signal, however, ISR exhibits Goodhart collapse at every tested dose: tautological constructions receive high ISR by construction, and any transient quality improvement disappears under extended training.
6	Across six reward conditions, two logic types, and two model scales, we identify a taxonomy of eight hacking modes and show that ISR is reliable as a diagnostic but unreliable as an optimization target.\footnote{Code and data: \url{[anonymized]}}
7	\end{abstract}
8

1	\section{Introduction}
2	\label{sec:introduction}
3	
4	Prove rate, the dominant evaluation metric for solver-backed formalizers~\citep{logic-lm, linc, satlm}, conflates faithful reasoning with degenerate shortcuts.
5	This gap is especially consequential under RL, where the optimizer exploits any shortcut the reward fails to penalize~\citep{skalse-reward-hacking, reward-hacking-survey}.
6	Recent work applies GRPO~\citep{deepseekmath} to train LLMs that generate solver-verified formal programs~\citep{mellgren-prolog-grpo, logic-rl} using binary execution feedback, driving rapid prove-rate gains but providing no signal on structural quality.
7	Process reward models~\citep{lightman-verify-step, math-shepherd} require expensive annotations and do not transfer to arbitrary solver-backed settings; a separate line of work documents that LLMs already game logical formalization at inference time~\citep{kim-game-formalization, bondarenko-spec-gaming}.
8	What is missing is an annotation-free \emph{diagnostic} for structural integrity in solver-backed formal outputs.
9	
10	We address this gap with \emph{Solver Trace Ablation} (STA), which applies leave-one-out ablation on solver inputs to test whether each component of a proved formalization is individually necessary (Figure~\ref{fig:sta-overview}).
11	The fraction of necessary components yields the \emph{Irreducible Solver Rate} (ISR), where $\text{ISR}{=}1$ indicates full necessity and $\text{ISR}{=}0$ complete redundancy.
12	LOO ablation itself is standard~\citep{demillo1978, jia-harman-survey}; our contribution lies in applying it as a GRPO-hacking diagnostic and in the empirical phenomena it surfaces.
13	As a post-hoc diagnostic on a GRPO formalizer trained with FOLIO~\citep{folio}, ISR reveals a structural collapse trajectory invisible to prove rate: structural quality degrades by $99.5\%$ over 1000 steps while prove rate rises from $32\%$ to $96\%$ (\S\ref{sec:diagnostic}).
14	An SFT baseline on identical data largely a

_source line 975 · 2026-05-18T11:23:23.863093+00:00_

1	\section{Related Work}
2	\label{sec:related-work}
3	
4	\paragraph{Formal reasoning with LLMs.}
5	LLM-solver pipelines~\citep{satlm, logic-lm, linc, fover-pei2025, lorp} and RL methods trained on solver feedback~\citep{polu2020generative, mellgren-prolog-grpo, logic-rl, deepseek-prover-v15, deepseek-prover-v2, deepseek-r1-2025} evaluate execution accuracy alone, with no diagnostic for structural quality.
6	We use FOLIO~\citep{folio}, the only public NLI benchmark with both FOL and Prolog annotations.
7	
8	\paragraph{Process rewards and reward shaping.}
9	Learned PRMs~\citep{lightman-verify-step, math-shepherd} provide step-level feedback but suffer from reward-model noise and distribution shift~\citep{processbench}; recent work extends PRMs to multi-domain settings~\citep{zeng2025versaprm} and process-level reasoning~\citep{khalifa2025thinkprm}. \citet{uesato2022solving} and \citet{gao-effective-rl-reward} show that auxiliary process/outcome rewards can \emph{hurt} RL training relative to success-only reward, a finding our ISR-as-reward results corroborate.
10	Unlike potential-based reward shaping~\citep{ng1999policy}, ISR augmentation does not preserve optimal policy, as it rewards output properties (component necessity) rather than state transitions, explaining the observed Goodhart vulnerability.
11	Symbolic feedback approaches~\citep{rlsf} use solver certificates as token-level rewards, achieving gains on program synthesis; ISR differs in operating at the component level (LOO ablation) rather than the token level, and our results show this structural signal is gamed when optimized.
12	Concurrent work by \citet{cao2026pushing} uses interleaved formal-logic verification bonuses during training (7B/14B models), dynamically penalizing intermediate fallacies; their verification-as-bonus approach faces similar Goodhart risks, though interleaving may mitigate the tautological collapse we observe under post-hoc ISR reward.
13	Rubric-grounded GRPO~\citep{rubric-grpo} us

1	\section{Method}
2	\label{sec:method}
3	
4	\begin{figure*}[t]
5	\centering
6	\includegraphics[width=0.92\textwidth]{figures/fig1_sta_overview.pdf}
7	\caption{Solver Trace Ablation (STA). (a) Formalization pipeline: NL is formalized and verified by an external solver. (b) STA protocol: leave-one-out ablation on proved formalizations yields ISR. (c) ISR as diagnostic vs.\ as reward: the diagnostic becomes the optimization target, exhibiting Goodhart effects.}
8	\label{fig:sta-overview}
9	\end{figure*}
10	
11	\subsection{Task and GRPO Training}
12	\label{sec:task}
13	
14	Given premises $P_1,\ldots,P_k$ and conclusion $C$, the task is to produce a formal program $F=\{c_1,\ldots,c_n\}$ verified by an external solver $S\in\{\text{Prover9, SWI-Prolog}\}$ (Figure~\ref{fig:sta-overview}a).
15	We train with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)~\citep{deepseekmath} ($K{=}4$), where each rollout receives binary execution reward $r_{\text{exec}}(x) = \mathbf{1}[S(F_x){=}\textit{proved}]$.
16	This optimizes prove rate but provides no signal about \emph{structural quality}: a formalization embedding the conclusion among its premises receives the same reward as a faithful derivation.
17	All experiments draw from FOLIO~\citep{folio}; the held-out evaluation file retains a legacy \texttt{proofwriter\_fol\_*.jsonl} filename (from ProofWriter~\citep{proofwriter}) but contains only FOLIO-derived examples.
18	
19	\subsection{Solver Trace Ablation (STA)}
20	\label{sec:sta}
21	
22	STA tests whether each component of a proved formalization is individually necessary for proof success (Figure~\ref{fig:sta-overview}b).
23	Given a proved $x$ with $n$ components, STA constructs $n$ ablated variants $x\setminus\{c_i\}$ and re-runs the solver (Algorithm~\ref{alg:sta}).
24	A component is \emph{necessary} if its removal causes proof failure.
25	What constitutes a component depends on the logic: premises and conclusion sub-expressions (quantifier, antecedent, consequent) in FOL, clauses or gro
…[truncated 6822 chars]

1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	We evaluate STA along three axes: ISR as a diagnostic for reward hacking (\S\ref{sec:diagnostic}); ISR as a training reward across four dose levels, where it exhibits Goodhart effects (\S\ref{sec:goodhart}); and STA as a cross-logic protocol (\S\ref{sec:crosslogic}).
5	Cross-scale evidence from Qwen3-1.7B reveals capacity-dependent Goodhart basins (\S\ref{sec:crossscale}).
6	A hacking taxonomy synthesizes the modes observed across conditions (\S\ref{sec:taxonomy}).
7	
8	\subsection{Setup}\label{sec:setup}
9	
10	We train Qwen3-4B~\citep{qwen3} with GRPO~\citep{deepseekmath} ($K{=}4$, lr $10^{-6}$, 500 steps default, 1000 where noted) over seeds $\{42,43,44\}$ on a fixed 500-example FOLIO~\citep{folio} subset held constant across all conditions; the SFT baseline is trained with negative log-likelihood (NLL) on gold formalizations.\footnote{All experiments use \texttt{data/train\_500\_seed42.jsonl}; seeds control initialization and rollout sampling only.}
11	Held-out evaluation uses 100 FOLIO examples (\texttt{proofwriter\_fol\_100.jsonl}) with greedy decoding (Appendix~\ref{app:setup}); training uses sampling ($T{=}0.7$), so evaluation reflects the mode of the learned distribution rather than the stochastic rollout behavior that drives RL updates.
12	We report prove rate (PR), proved-only ISR ($\text{ISR}^*$), and hacking-mode rates from a two-stage classifier (\S\ref{sec:taxonomy-method}): strict text matching for H1/H7, followed by functional analysis of $n_{\text{necessary}}$.
13	The \emph{non-hacking} (N-H) rate is the residual of proved samples matching none of the eight automatically cataloged patterns; a ninth mode, H9, is identified by manual audit (\S\ref{sec:taxonomy}).
14	\emph{Strict non-hacking} further requires content-dependent predicates derived from the natural-language premises, filtering content-independent proofs that evade the taxonomy through structural novelty (\S\ref{sec:crossscale}).

1	\section{Discussion and Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	
4	STA exposes structural degeneration invisible to execution-based evaluation (\S\ref{sec:diagnostic}), and ISR exhibits Goodhart collapse at all four tested doses (\S\ref{sec:goodhart}--\ref{sec:crossscale}).
5	At 500 steps, ISR produces descriptively more non-hacking output than random controls (Table~\ref{tab:goodhart-landscape}), but seed-level permutation tests are underpowered ($n{=}5$), and the advantage reverses under stricter classification.
6	Extended training confirms the window closes.
7	The core asymmetry is clear: the same LOO structure that makes ISR a useful diagnostic also makes it a vulnerable optimization target, because tautological constructions satisfy component necessity by definition.
8	While Goodhart collapse under LOO-based metrics is theoretically predictable, the specific dynamics resist prediction from the metric definition alone: seed-dependent divergence, dose-varying collapse patterns, and cross-logic mode divergence each required empirical observation to identify.
9	
10	All experiments use a single model family (Qwen3-4B/1.7B), one dataset (FOLIO), and one RL algorithm (GRPO).
11	The specific hacking modes and their relative prevalence may differ under other architectures or formal reasoning benchmarks, but the structural vulnerability of LOO-based metrics to tautological exploits follows from the metric definition and should transfer to any setting where the optimizer can reduce component count.
12	Our results illustrate a concrete instance of Goodhart's law in RL for formal reasoning~\citep{manheim-goodhart-taxonomy}: the failure mode is regressional rather than extremal, with the optimizer concentrating probability mass on regions where ISR's structural bias coincides with degenerate outputs.
13	For scalable oversight of solver-backed systems, structural diagnostics should remain decoupled from the training loop.
14

1	\section{Limitations}
2	\label{sec:limitations}
3	
4	Experiments use a single model family (Qwen3-4B/1.7B), a single structured-NLI dataset (FOLIO, 500 training examples and 100 evaluation examples), and a single RL algorithm (GRPO).
5	Whether the specific hacking modes and collapse dynamics generalize to other architectures or larger-scale formal reasoning benchmarks remains open.
6	Our 1.7B experiments use three seeds per condition; strict non-hacking rates range from $0$--$3.7\%$ (ISR $\beta{=}0.1$) to $0$--$29\%$ (ISR $\beta{=}0.3$, driven by a single low-PR seed), and $N{=}3$ may miss rare non-hacking-capable initializations.
7	The automated classifier does not detect H9 (ex falso) at 1.7B; manual audit found H9 instances within taxonomy-classified categories, so 1.7B non-hacking rates may overestimate true rates.
8	
9	ISR quantifies syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness: a high-ISR proof can still be logically vacuous if its individually necessary premises are collectively inconsistent or semantically ungrounded.
10	A downstream NLI evaluation confirms this gap ($\rho{=}{-}0.049$, $p{=}0.187$; Appendix~\ref{app:extended-discussion}).
11	The current decomposition treats conclusion sub-expressions (quantifier, antecedent, consequent) as separate components; for tautological formulas where sub-expressions overlap with premises, ISR values can depend on decomposition granularity rather than proof structure, though these edge cases do not affect aggregate statistics.
12	Non-hacking is a residual classification: undiscovered gaming modes would reduce the true rate, as illustrated by H7b (obfuscated tautology evading text-match classifiers) and query-as-fact gaming in Prolog.
13	STA requires solvers supporting component-level input manipulation, adding $O(n)$ solver calls per sample (mean $n{\approx}6$--$8$ in our experiments, ${\sim}6$--$8{\times}$ evaluation overhead); at training time with ISR reward, this overhead applies to all proved rollou
…[truncated 1353 chars]

1	\section{Ethics Statement}
2	\label{sec:ethics}
3	
4	This work documents failure modes of reward optimization in solver-backed RL systems.
5	Our findings (that structural diagnostics can be gamed when used as training signals) carry implications for AI safety: they demonstrate that metrics designed to detect misalignment may themselves become targets of optimization, a concern relevant to scalable oversight.
6	We release our taxonomy and diagnostic tools to help practitioners detect reward hacking in their own systems.
7	All experiments use publicly available datasets (FOLIO) and open-weight models (Qwen3-4B); no human subjects are involved.
8
